# `cards-after-marriage` — "Is there a soulmate for me after the marriage ended?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 55-64 BINARY rung.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 NEVER PRESUME HOW THE MARRIAGE ENDED. 'The marriage ended' covers divorce AND death, and the theme pull (sm-after-marriage, 2026-08-19) returned both — 21 buyers, most divorced, and a real bereaved slice ('lost many loved ones recently, starting over at this age'). So: no 'your divorce', no 'when he left', no 'since he passed', no 'your ex'. The read holds for a widow and for a woman who walked out, or it is wrong for half the traffic. 🔴 NO MEDIUMSHIP, for the same reason.

🔴 NO RULING ON THE MAN and NO BLAME ON HER. The same pull is full of abuse, fraud and financial ruin — 'he got very abusive towards me in the end', 'extorted me 8 months with divorcing papers', 'left me with no finances and no home'. Nothing about what she should have done, what she attracted, or what the marriage taught her about herself.

⚠ Meta-sensitive material sits right under this headline: financial hardship and health both surface in the pull. The read goes nowhere near either.

🔴 NO DURATION and NO DATE (see cards-keeps-waiting). The hopeful YES is allowed and is the answer she came for — the directional loosening of 2026-08-19 covers it — but it may never be attached to a time, a place, or a person.

The finding: she has been counting to one. One marriage, one chance, and the sum stops there.

# `cards-allowed-to-want` — "Am I still allowed to want a soulmate?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 65+ WHY rung. ⭐ The operator's own line, and the only one in the batch that offers PERMISSION rather than a prediction.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The word doing the work is ALLOWED. She is not asking for a forecast, so this is the one lander in the batch whose cut 3 must NOT answer 'yes, they're coming' — answering the wrong question here throws away the only thing that makes the headline different. The theme pull (sm-allowed-to-want, 2026-08-19) also names who took the permission away: 'Friends tell me I'm too old to want a man, but I've been widowed for six years and would like a good man', 'I sometimes feel silly thinking I will have another love at my age', 'At my age, I suppose I should just be content', 'Older mother and adult children with too much to say ...especially at my age .65'.

🔴🔴 NO PREDICTION, NO ARRIVAL, NO TIMING. Not because the directional loosening forbids it — it doesn't — but because it answers a question she did not ask, and because the same pull contains an active romance scam and a woman left destitute at 82. A promised arrival is the sentence a scammer's mark can act on.

🔴🔴 NOTHING ABOUT HER LOOKS, HER BODY, OR HER AGE. 'I've gained weight and I'm so much older', 'I have a hard time accepting what age has done to my face and body..I look very old', 'I don't have my youthful look, I feel very unattractive'. A 'you're still beautiful' line is a lie she can check in a mirror, and it is platform-flagged material besides. No number, no face, no body, ever.

🔴🔴 NO MEDIUMSHIP. This pull is the most bereaved of the eleven — 'the love of my life died after 38 years together', 'widowed for six years', 'my partner died 10 years ago', 'my 45 year old daughter passed unexpectedly'. Never presume a death, never speak for one, never 'he'd want you to'.

🔴 NEVER NAME WHO WITHHELD THE PERMISSION. Friends, an older mother, adult children are all named in the pull. Naming a real person as the block is banned funnel-wide. Cut 7 asks WHAT taught her to ask, never WHO.

🔴 DO NOT GENDER THE SOULMATE. Men are in this pull ('will I ever meet her', 'I'm still a Virgin at my age') and so is a same-sex partnership of 38 years. No he, no him, no man, anywhere in the read.

🔴 NO HEALTH AND NO MONEY. Hip replacement, PTSD, 'medically I have to avoid stressful situations', destitution and scam loss all sit in the pull.

⚠ Cut 6 DESCRIBES, it does not certify. 'You have every right to want this' is the psychic ruling on her and it is the exact move the framework bans — hand her back her own week instead.

The finding: nobody hands this permission out, so nobody could have taken it back. She has been waiting to be told, and the wanting has picked up an apology on the way.

# `cards-alone-forever` — "Will I be alone forever?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FATE, IN ANY FORM — 'you are meant to be alone' is the most harmful sentence available on this funnel, and 'it is meant to be', 'the universe has a plan', 'this is preparing you', 'there is a reason for this' are the same sentence in kinder clothes: they make her suffering purposeful and assign it to her. 🔴 NO LIFE SENTENCE and 🔴 NO PROMISE — 'you will always be alone' and 'you will meet someone' are both strangers ruling on the rest of her life. 🔴 No pathologising: nothing about her being negative, bitter, closed off, self-sabotaging, or 'attracting what she puts out'.

🔴 BOTH ANSWERS ARE FORBIDDEN, so every card must VISIBLY DECLINE the absolute — a quiet sidestep reads as evasion rather than as a reading. The finding that replaces the verdict: 'forever' is not a length. It is what the WEIGHT feels like from underneath, and the weight is the real thing to name.

# `cards-back-together` — "Will we get back together?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The US-FRAMED sibling of `reunion`, and that pairing is the whole experiment — reunion casts him as the agent and her as the one waiting; these cast the relationship as a joint thing with two people in it.

🔴 THE FORBIDDEN VERDICT IS ON THE RELATIONSHIP, NOT ON HIM — harder, not softer. A claim about a man can at least be measured against his behaviour; 'it is over' or 'it is not over' is unfalsifiable to her and lands as a ruling on her life from someone who has never met either of them. 🔴 NO ODDS — cards-still-a-chance asks for a number outright and there is none; no percentage, no 'strong chance', no 'likely'. 🔴 NO DIRECTIVES in either direction: move on, let go, fight for him, reach out first are all out. 🔴 Never hand her the outcome ('it is up to you', 'if you had only') — the us-framing makes that easy and it lands as blame.

Finding, and the one that is truest to the us-framing: this question is about a JOINT thing and only one of the two people is in the room. She has been carrying a two-person decision on her own — which is why it will not resolve however long she turns it over.

# `cards-best-years` — "Why did I give my best years to someone who wasn't my soulmate?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 55-64 WHY rung.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The words doing the work are GAVE and BEST YEARS — she is the one who spent, and what she spent cannot be got back. The theme pull (sm-best-years, 2026-08-19) is blunt about it: 'I feel like I've wasted my life loving him', 'I've wasted the last 20 years', 'I invested 30 years of my life, loved him with all my heart, I just don't exist to him', 'I feel sad I wasted my best years on him'.

🔴🔴 SHE IS OFTEN STILL IN IT. The headline is past tense and a large share of the pull is present tense — 'married 37 years to a man I no longer have a connection to', 'married for 35 years to a man who doesn't really value me', 'I am married but there is no love there... I can't leave', 'Living 50 years with someone who's only ever taken'. So: no 'now that it's over', no 'now you're free', no 'that chapter is closed', and NOTHING that tells her to leave. The read must hold for a woman going back to that house tonight.

🔴🔴 NO LESSON, NO PURPOSE MADE OF IT. The finding says she still holds what she became in those years — it may NEVER be phrased as the years having happened in order to teach her. 'It was all for a reason' is the loneliness family's banned move (suffering made purposeful) and it is worse here, because the pull is full of abuse, fraud and a man she visited in prison and funded every week.

🔴 NO BLAME AND NO RULING ON HIM. Domestic abuse, financial abuse, cheating and criminality are all in the pull. Nothing about what she allowed, what she attracted, why she stayed, or what kind of man he was.

🔴 NO MEDIUMSHIP — 'Widowed over a year now after almost 40 years with a man' is in the pull. 🔴 NO HEALTH, NO MONEY ADVICE, NO AGE NUMBERS ('I'm 71 and feel I have led a wasted life', 'now with health problems it's hard to make changes').

🔴 DISTRESS IS IN THIS PULL — 'The only thing that's keeping me alive is a promise God made to me 18 years ago... I'm actually starting to hate life itself.' The read may not deepen the it-was-all-for-nothing reading. Cut 3 refuses the void flat, on every card.

⚠ MUST NOT RESTATE `cards-choosing-wrong`, which runs on the same age band and the same wrong man. That lander answers HOW SHE PICKED; this one answers WHAT THE TIME COST. Keep the mechanism out of it.

The finding: the years were not paid out to him. What she built inside them is still on her, and the thing that hurts is the count she keeps running, not the man.

# `cards-blocked-retiring` — "Why is my money still blocked this close to retiring?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Money translation: there is no man here, so cut 4 becomes what the block has been DOING, cut 5 becomes the EARNING against the KEEPING, and cut 6 names the near-miss she has lived — the VOC's own sentence. 🔴 NONE of the seven bans below was loosened with the love families; this is the one place where a wrong sentence costs her actual money.

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

✅ GUARDED as of 2026-08-19: `tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts` now exists and runs against these drafts DIRECTLY. It had to: the drafts setup can only patch a hook the registry already has, so an unwired family is checked by nothing at all and still prints a green tick. All seven bans were fed a deliberate violation and all seven fired.

📊 WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. These hooks have never run, so there is no per-hook data. The nearest real corpus is docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md — 10,514 V1 money concerns. Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions.
**Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.'
**The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.'
So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go.

Tendency: her ability was never the problem, and something stands between the EARNING and the KEEPING. Never name what or who, never a sum, a date or a source, never suggest anything be done with money.

4. And look where he stands. The trouble sits further down the line than his hands.
5. But earning a thing and keeping it are two different jobs, dear.
6. That's why it goes just as it's about to land in your hands.
7. Let me look closer at what steps in right at the landing…

</details>

3. So the money is held, dear. Held is a long way from gone.
4. And the rope has him at one point only. The rest of him hangs free.
5. But one rope is enough to stop the whole of him moving.
6. That's why the other parts of your life work and this one part never does.
7. Let me look closer at what has had hold of that one point…

# `cards-cant-stop` — "Why can't I stop thinking about him?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO DIRECTIVE — 'let him go', 'time to move on', 'try to forget him' are what she has been told by everyone already, and they are not a reading. 🔴 No promise about him and 🔴 no pathologising: obsessed, unhealthy, 'you are stuck' are all banned. 🔴 No timeframe.

Finding, kept distinct from its two siblings: the word 'stop'. She has framed her own mind as a machine she is failing to switch off, and has been trying to win by force. Minds do not take orders — and the trying is costing more than the thinking.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — both his hands are busy and neither is resting.
2. You asked why you can't stop thinking about him. Your hand went to the man who can't put it down.
3. So you've been trying to stop by force, dear. That's the tiring bit.
4. And his hands are busy, dear, with no off switch anywhere on him.
5. But a mind doesn't take orders like that, dear. Not one.
6. That's why the trying costs you more than the thinking does, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept you fighting your own head…

# `cards-cheating` — "Is he cheating on you?"

⛔ The decode-him DECK was retired 2026-08-19 (operator call) — its strip art was the fb-palm thumb image and no ad ever pointed at it. Its block has been removed from this draft; the arcana and return-mhf decks are unaffected.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 THIS FAMILY WAS DELIBERATELY NOT LOOSENED: cut 3 still answers her FEAR (am I short of sense, or short of facts) and never rules on him in either direction. The art line and the bridge are unchanged.

Lives on ALL FOUR decks — three card-sets: Magician/Hanged Man/Fool (return-mhf + arcana-mfh, one copy, verb swapped), Emperor/Empress/Fool (arcana-eef), Sun/Moon/Tower (decode-him). The arcana-eef Fool reuses the MHF Fool copy verb-swapped.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD IS AVOIDED IN EVERY BEAT — 'cheat' and its forms appear nowhere, which is what the live copy on three of the four decks already does (decode-him has one slip today; this rewrite removes it). The ad headline carries the word; the read does not. The echo still lands because the gate keys on 'you'.

🔴 NEVER SAY HE HAS OR HAS NOT. TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY: 'that the unease she feels is real information about her situation, not paranoia to apologize for. NEVER say he is cheating or that he is faithful as fact.' So what gets certified is her UNEASE — a signal she has been apologising for — and what gets named is that no answer was ever put beside it.

This hook is the INCUMBENT the whole fidelity family is tested against: tests/tarot-fidelity-copy.test.ts requires those four landers to share no 6-word run with it on any deck. Its own finding is kept distinct from all four — unease as a real signal, where cards-someone-else takes the GAP, cards-loyal the PORTION, cards-talking-someone the ATTENTION, cards-faithful the REST.

🔴 decode-him takes no picture line — its strip is the fb-PALM thumb art and there is no revealStrip, so there is no card for her to look at. Beat 1 uses the card-name form, and all three are written fresh (all four hooks on that deck currently share one).

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his table stands on the grass, out in the open.
2. You asked if he has been true to you. Your hand went to the man who sets the scene.
3. So the distance is real, dear. I will not talk you out of it.
4. And a scene like that gets set on purpose, dear. Look at his table.
5. But what's set out isn't the same as what's so, dear.
6. That's why you can sit through a fine evening, dear, and still feel outside it.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and the plain view…

# `cards-choosing-wrong` — "What keeps me choosing everyone but my soulmate?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 25-44 WHY rung.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE HEADLINE PRESUPPOSES SHE CHOSE, AND THE VOC SAYS SHE OFTEN DID NOT. The theme pull (sm-choosing-wrong, 2026-08-19) came back as fraud and abuse: 'badly hurt by a man I thought loved me but he was a scammer', 'my marriage broke up because of domestic abuse and I'm scared of yet again choosing a wrong man', 'abusive, and divorced him', 'he turned out to be a convicted paedophile'. Telling a woman who was defrauded or assaulted that she keeps choosing wrong blames her for a crime committed against her.

So cut 3 REFUSES THE PREMISE, on every card. She did not choose them; she was shown something else — her own words: 'seemed to be one person but changed along the way'. 🔴 NOTHING about a pattern in her, low self-worth, attachment style, healing herself first, attracting what she is, or needing to fix anything before love arrives. 🔴 NO diagnosis of any kind. 🔴 Never name or convict a real man either — the read describes what was SHOWN, never rules on who he is.

🔴 NO DURATION, NO DATE, NO PROMISED ARRIVAL.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the sword on his table has a cross at the handle.
2. You asked what keeps you choosing everyone but your soulmate. Your hand went to the man with his tools laid out.
3. So you didn't choose them, dear. They showed you one man and turned into another.
4. And that table has four different things on it, dear. You got shown one.
5. But you had one thing to go on, dear. Anyone would have read it the same.
6. That's why it changed on you later, dear. You'd only ever seen the front.
7. Let me look closer at what gets shown first and what comes after…

> 🆕 **New lander** — nothing to compare against; this hook is not in the registry yet.

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his shoes are gold, and they're the brightest thing he wears.
2. You asked what keeps you choosing everyone but your soulmate. Your hand went to the card that hangs the wrong way up.
3. So the fault was not in your choosing, dear. You were shown the wrong way up.
4. And the gold on this card sits at his feet, dear. Not at his head.
5. But you can only see what's turned toward you, dear. The rest stays round the back.
6. That's why they seemed right at the start, dear. The bright part faces out.
7. Let me look closer at what has been turned away from you each time…

> 🆕 **New lander** — nothing to compare against; this hook is not in the registry yet.

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the flowers on his coat are stitched, not picked.
2. You asked what keeps you choosing everyone but your soulmate. Your hand went to the one dressed for the road.
3. So you were not picking badly, dear. You were picking from what came near you.
4. And the flowers on that coat are stitched on, dear. Not one of them grew.
5. But from where you stood they looked real, dear. Anyone would think so.
6. That's why you believed them, dear. They were made to look that way.
7. Let me look closer at what was sewn on before you ever met…

> 🆕 **New lander** — nothing to compare against; this hook is not in the registry yet.

# `cards-come-back` — "Will he come back?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FORECAST IN EITHER DIRECTION — 'he will come back' and 'he has moved on' are both things no card knows, and the second is the one that sounds like honesty. 🔴 Never blame her for waiting: naive, too trusting, foolish, clinging, desperate and 'wasted your time' are all banned outright. 🔴 No timeframe, no guarantee.

⚠️ SHARES ITS HEADLINE WITH cards-return ON PURPOSE — the two are a deliberate A/B on the same ad question, so this read has to be genuinely its own or the comparison is dead. cards-return's finding is that he is not short of a way back and the pull is not only hers; this one takes the DOOR SHE HAS BEEN HOLDING — the waiting is a job she has been doing alone with no one to relieve her.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the roses above him are wide open, not in bud.
2. You asked about him coming back. Your hand went to the man who does the doing.
3. So this hasn't closed, dear. The door you've held is still open.
4. And it's one person doing all the holding, dear. Look at him.
5. But holding a door open takes it out of you, dear. Every day.
6. That's why you're worn thin, dear. Nobody offered to take a turn.
7. Let me look closer at what has been wedged in that doorway…

# `cards-deceived` — "Am I being deceived?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT IN EITHER DIRECTION. These three name an untruth outright, so both answers are live harms: 'he lied to you' is an accusation against a real man on no evidence, and 'he is telling you the truth' is a reassurance the funnel has no standing to give — and on this audience the second is the more dangerous of the two. 🔴 Never blame her judgment: naive, gullible, too trusting, 'should have known' are all out. 🔴 No timeframe.

Finding: 'deceived' is a heavier word than 'lied to' — it means a whole picture, not one sentence. She is asking whether the shape of the thing is what she thinks. ⚠️ Its beat 2 could not restate her headline: 'you are being deceived' is itself a banned phrase, so the echo is built as 'you asked about being deceived'.

# `cards-energy-how-long` — "How long has my energy been working against my money?"

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free.

✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all).

Tendency: her energy has not been working against her at all — refuse the premise gently — and whatever settled on this settled long ago and was never named. 🔴 NEVER give a length of time and never date it to an event in her life.

Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 NOT LOOSENED: no verdict on a third person in EITHER direction, so cut 3 answers her FEAR (was something real taken from me, or am I making it up) and never rules on him. Calling her it is the injury; using it to reassure her plants it. That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words.

🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. Never instruct her to check a phone, gather proof, or confront him — the most available wrong answer to these questions anywhere on the internet. Never excuse him, never land it as her fault. Four separate findings, or the family is one lander wearing four headlines.

# `cards-feels` — "How does he really feel about you?"

⛔ The decode-him DECK was retired 2026-08-19 (operator call) — its strip art was the fb-palm thumb image and no ad ever pointed at it. Its block has been removed from this draft; the arcana and return-mhf decks are unaffected.

Card-sets differ by deck, so the copy does too: Magician/Hanged Man/Fool (return-mhf + arcana-mfh), Sun/Moon/Tower (decode-him), Emperor/Empress/Fool (arcana-eef). The two Magician decks share one set, verb swapped. 🔴 decode-him's beat 1s are written fresh rather than kept: all four hooks on that deck currently open with the SAME line per card (no guard file catches it — see the duplication note in the checklist).

🔴 BEAT 4 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 — it must name an OBSTRUCTION, not an absence. Act 1 of the trilogy sells an Energy Clearing Ritual that removes "the shadow that's been blocking your path"; improve-v1/08-clearing-theme-coherence.md found that clearing is SPRUNG at the pitch rather than seeded. This is where it gets seeded, and the object goes between HER and what she wants — the love bucket frames every block as an impersonal thing in her path precisely so removing it blames nobody. "What he never said" cannot be cleared; "what's sitting between you and the words" can.

🔴 BEAT 3 CORRECTED 2026-08-19. tests/tarot-real-feelings-copy.test.ts fired — a deliberate tripwire on this hook, because cards-feels is the CONTROL for two comparisons. The first rewrite dropped the old copy's "leans toward" hedge to reach grade 5, which turned a tendency into a flat verdict on his heart ("He does feel it, dear") — exactly what this hook's TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY bans. Plain English did not require it: "You haven't been reading coldness, dear. You've been reading a hold." says the same thing at the same grade, about HER reading rather than his interior. Rule applied: no sentence may take "he" as the subject of a feeling verb stated as fact; negations ("doesn't mean he's cold") and card-tendency forms ("it reads as") are fine.

# `cards-feels-off` — "Something feels off — is my intuition right?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER CONVICT HIM and 🔴 never dismiss what she noticed — both doors are shut. 🔴🔴 AND NEVER NAME CONTENTS: no other woman, no affair, no money, no secret family, no messages from anyone. Naming a content is the single most harmful thing this pair could do, because she will go looking for it. 🔴 No surveillance and no 'confront him'.

⚠️ THIS HOOK SUBMITS HER OWN JUDGEMENT FOR A VERDICT, which is a trap: 'yes, you are right' convicts him through her, and 'no, you are wrong' teaches her to distrust the one instrument she has. So the question is SPLIT rather than answered — the read certifies that she registered something real, and leaves what it means entirely open. (The word 'intuition' is four syllables and cannot appear at grade 5; the echo lands on 'feels', 'off' and 'right' instead.)

4. And the coin is easy to miss, dear, and it's still there.
5. But noticing is one job, dear. Reading it is another.
6. That's why the first part is done, dear, and the second is still open.
7. Let me look closer at what your eye caught on…

# `cards-forever-or-now` — "Am I his forever, or his now?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened.

FACE-DOWN ONLY (return-mhf) — tests/tarot-his-other-life-copy.test.ts pins this family off the face-up decks.

🔴 REFUSES ITS BINARY ON ITS OWN GROUNDS, and the test enforces it: beat 3 must still carry the permanence-is-BUILT finding (`not a condition` / `is built` / `only been felt` / `no such fact` / `never been named`), and must share no 6-word run with cards-moved-on, cards-imagining-it or cards-love-or-moved-on. Both poles stay banned — never "you are his forever" (a promise), never "you are only his now" (a dismissal).

VOC: this family is 5 days old and has 14 quotes across all five hooks, so voc-by-hook could not do the job. Read 769 real V1 concerns on the married-man/other-woman theme instead (new scripts/voc-by-theme.mjs). What she types: "he calls me his wife but there is no actions", "I'm tired of not being chosen", "I will no longer be a secret in his life", "he presented as though his relationship with his wife was over". She has felt forever and never once been TOLD it. So the payoff certifies the feeling and the gap names the silence — she has been reading a silence as a verdict already passed on her.

# `cards-found-me-yet` — "Why hasn't my soulmate found me yet?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 45-54 WHY rung.

⚠ THINNEST EVIDENCE IN THE BATCH, and the operator shipped it knowing that. The theme pull (sm-not-found-me, 2026-08-19) matched FOUR concerns in the whole corpus and ZERO buyers. Nobody phrases it as him finding her — she says 'why haven't I found love', 'why haven't I met that special person yet'. The agency flip is the AD's idea, so the read is written to her fear and not to the flip.

Her fear is on record, verbatim, from the how-long pull: 'How much longer must I wait to meet him? HAVE I DONE SOMETHING TO PREVENT OUR MEETING?' That is what cut 3 answers on every card — nothing she did stopped this.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO DURATION, in any form. Every frame on this funnel bans a DATE; four headlines in this batch ask HOW LONG, and 'not much longer' or 'a few years' is a duration, not a date, so it walks straight through the date ban. Banned here as its own thing: no length of time, forward or back, no season, no 'soon', no 'not long now'.

🔴 NO SELF-BLAME and NO STRATEGY — the word doing the work is YET, and the two answers the internet gives ('you're not ready' / 'get out more') are the two this read exists to refuse. 🔴 No PLACE (the soulmate-where gap) and no promised arrival on a clock.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the cup on his table has a stem and a foot.
2. You asked why your soulmate hasn't found you yet. Your hand went to the man with his work set out.
3. So you did nothing to stop this, dear. Not one thing you did closed it off.
4. And the cup on that table is already out, dear. Filled and standing.
5. But you've read that as something you failed at, dear.
6. That's why you turn it over so often, dear. You were ready, and it still didn't come.
7. Let me look closer at what's been left standing in front of that cup…

The reason lives in a man who did not give it, and inventing one hands her something false to act on. 🔴 Never a verdict on his person, 🔴 never invent a third person, and 🔴 note that 'it was never about you' and 'he was protecting you' are banned too — they are motive claims wearing reassurance. 🔴 No tactic in either direction: no reaching out, no one last message, no blocking, no no-contact. 🔴 No promise of closure.

Finding: she has been handed a silence and asked to author its meaning. The read refuses to author it for her and names the work she has been doing instead.

# `cards-her-shadow` — "Am I living in her shadow?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened.

⚠️ "her" may be an EX or a woman who has DIED, and the headline does not say. 🔴 NEVER presume which. 🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP BAN (this family runs the decode-him frame, which carries none of it): never that she is at peace, watching, or would want anything. 🔴 NEVER disparage or characterise her, never make her a rival, never rank the two women. And the tightrope: never confirm the comparison as fact, never tell her she is imagining it.

VOC (769 V1 concerns, married-man/other-woman theme) settles why that tightrope is real: the other woman is very often NOT a rival — she is dead, ill, dependent, or entirely legitimate. "He lost his wife of 57 years a short while ago." "His wife is in bad health... I certainly would not hurt his wife." "I'm not a marriage breaker." Handing this reader an enemy would be harm dressed as comfort.

So the split, exactly as `hidden-intuition` splits its question: certify HER FEELING (she has felt something in the room) and refuse to rule on the woman at all. The finding does the rest — a shadow is not a person, it is what falls when something blocks the light. Which makes bubble 7 the easiest object on the funnel: the thing blocking the light IS the block Act 1 clears.

# `cards-his-children` — "Why do his children come before me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened.

🔴🔴 THE SHARPEST LANDER ON THE FUNNEL. It asks a card to comment on a man's CHILDREN — real third parties, possibly minors, who cannot consent to being read. NEVER rank her against them in either direction, never characterise them, never grade him as a father. The test pins BOTH halves of the payout: beat 3 must say plainly that children DO have a first call (`first call`), AND must offer a DEFINED place rather than a higher one (`place of your own` / `where you sit` / `appear on one` / `planned for you`).

VOC (192 V1 concerns on the his-children theme): the injury is NOT rank. Not one woman asked to outrank a child. What they typed: "his daughter is getting married in July and I'm not invited... I've been Keith's partner for nine years", "he had a whole life without me", "I feel rejected from the family", "he lets them disrespect me". She is not on the list at all — in either direction. So the read says the true thing first (first call is real), then names the blur that actually hurts: first call was never the same as only call.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a gold cup and a coin marked with a star, set out in order.
2. You asked why his children come before you. Your hand went to the man who plans.
3. So his children do have a first call, dear. I won't dress that up for you.
4. And first call was never the only call, dear. Look how much is on that table.
5. But nobody has ever set a place there for you. Not one you could point at.
6. That's why you're not asking to move up a list, dear. You're asking to appear on one.

# `cards-honest` — "Is he being honest with you?"

⛔ The decode-him DECK was retired 2026-08-19 (operator call) — its strip art was the fb-palm thumb image and no ad ever pointed at it. Its block has been removed from this draft; the arcana and return-mhf decks are unaffected.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 THIS FAMILY WAS DELIBERATELY NOT LOOSENED: cut 3 still answers her FEAR (am I short of sense, or short of facts) and never rules on him in either direction. The art line and the bridge are unchanged.

Lives on ALL FOUR decks — three card-sets: Magician/Hanged Man/Fool (return-mhf + arcana-mfh, one copy, verb swapped), Emperor/Empress/Fool (arcana-eef), Sun/Moon/Tower (decode-him). The arcana-eef Fool reuses the MHF Fool copy verb-swapped, which is what the live registry already does for this hook.

🔴 decode-him takes NO picture line. Its strip art is the fb-PALM thumb image and there is no revealStrip, so there is no card for her to look at — beat 1 uses the card-name form instead, exactly as the already-shipped cards-feels and cards-return do on that deck. Every beat 1 here is written FRESH: all four decode-him hooks currently open with the SAME line per card and no guard file catches it.

🔴 NEVER ACQUIT HIM AND NEVER CONVICT HIM. TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY: 'never state he is lying or that he is honest as fact; affirm HER instrument'. The VOC makes that a safety rule rather than a style rule — 28 real concerns off this exact lander, and a large share of them have never met the man: 'I just want make sure he is not an impostor', 'he says he loves me... I haven't met him afraid he's a scammer', 'I met this guy online. He's from Nigeria... my gut feeling tells me he's not [coming]'. A line that vouches for him could cost one of these women money. So every beat 3 certifies HER NOTICING and names what she was never given — never a verdict in either direction.

The accusation the absolution refuses is hers from the VOC too: 'Left confused doubting our entire relationship with no answers', 'I don't know if I can trust him'. She has been told she is distrustful. She is short of facts.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he looks straight out at you, and his mouth is closed.
2. You asked if he's being honest with you. Your hand went to the man who tells it well.
3. So what you're short of is a fact, dear. Not sense, and not calm.
4. And look how he holds himself. He's very good at how a thing is put.
5. But a smooth account isn't proof either way, dear.
6. That's why his answers land fine, dear, and you still go back over them.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and the whole story…

# `cards-how-much-longer` — "How much longer will something keep blocking my money?"

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free.

✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all).

Tendency: a block does not run down like a clock — it lasts as long as it is left alone, so the honest answer is about what gets moved, not a number. 🔴 NEVER give a date, a timeframe or a season, even a soft one.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he could touch all four things without moving his feet.
2. You asked how much longer. Your hand went to the card of a person who acts.
3. So this isn't a wait, dear. It's a thing that has to be moved.
4. And every tool he needs is within reach, dear. Nothing is far off.
5. But no number from me, dear. A block doesn't run down like a clock.
6. That's why last year looked just like this one, dear. Nothing was touched.
7. Let me look closer at what has not been touched…

# `cards-imagining-it` — "Does he love me, or am I imagining it?"

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, after the interior ban was loosened. The read may now ANSWER: the card carries the claim about him. 🔴 THE DENIAL IS STILL BANNED — 'he does not love you' is the one direction she has no use for. 🔴 NO DIAGNOSIS: avoidant, emotionally unavailable, commitment-phobic, narcissist. 🔴 Never grade her (naive, foolish, gave too much). 🔴 No dates. The seven cuts run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why / let me look closer — and every cut must add information the last one did not carry. 🔴🔴 The crueller door stays shut: never that she imagined it, invented it or is seeing what she wants. 🔴 The warrant is the CARD, never the strength of what she felt.

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

2. You asked about his love, and about your own eyes. Your hand went to the card of things truly made.
3. So you did not imagine it, dear. He loves you.
4. And this card builds nothing by chance. What stands between you was made on purpose.
5. But he has put it into the doing, dear, and not into words you can hold.
6. That's why you keep checking it against your own memory.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps him from saying it plainly…

# `cards-keeps-waiting` — "How long does a soulmate keep you waiting?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 45-54 HOW-LONG rung.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 NO DURATION — the defining ban of this batch, and the reason four of these landers cannot run under any existing frame. Every frame on the funnel bans a DATE. This headline asks for a LENGTH, and 'not much longer', 'a few more months', 'soon', 'not long now' are all lengths rather than dates, so the date ban does not touch them. Banned outright: any span of time, any pace, any 'by then', any season, forward or back.

🔴 THE REFUSAL MUST BE VISIBLE, not a sidestep. Both answers are unavailable here, so a read that quietly changes the subject reads as evasion and she feels conned — same treatment as cards-alone-forever, which declines the length out loud and then gives her something real instead.

⚠ The corpus warning: in her own words 'how long' almost always means waiting on a SPECIFIC man ('how long should I wait for Jack'). This lander must not answer as though a named man exists. 🔴 No verdict on any real person, no promised arrival, no PLACE, no self-blame, no 'he is worth the wait'.

The finding that replaces the verdict: the tiredness is not from the length of the wait. It is from holding it open on her own.

🔴 NEVER SUPPLY A CAUSE for the separate homes — not that he is not serious, not that he is protecting himself, not money, not children, not an ex. The reason lives in him, and inventing one hands her something to act on. Its own test guard sweeps for exactly that. Never read the arrangement as a verdict on his feelings in either direction, never a timeframe, never an ultimatum, never tell her to move in or move out.

VOC (678 V1 concerns on the distance/living-apart theme) shows she has already HEARD the reasons — that is the point. "I've asked him to move in, but 'he can't right now financially.'" "He wants us to continue to grow together... how long should I wait?" "There is something not quite right with his home life but he doesn't discuss it with me." Every reason she has been given arrived without a date. So the read refuses the 'why' (his to give) and answers the shape instead: a plan and a habit look identical from outside, and nobody has told her which one she lives in. The absolution is the one she has never been offered — she has been doing the work of two people, living the arrangement AND translating it afterwards.

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 65+ HOW-LONG rung. ⚠ The hook is `cards-longer-to-wait`, NOT `cards-how-much-longer` — that name is already taken by a MONEY lander ('How much longer will something keep blocking my money?').

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 NO DURATION — see cards-keeps-waiting. She is asking for a length outright, and 'not much longer' is a length rather than a date, so the standing date ban does not catch it. Banned: any span, any 'soon', any 'not long now', any pace.

🔴 THIS IS THE MORTALITY-ADJACENT ONE. Her own words from the how-long pull: 'I'm 70 years old and not sure how much longer I have to wait?' At this age 'how much longer' carries 'is there enough time left' underneath it, and the read must answer the second question without ever touching the first. NO AGE, NO NUMBER, no 'while you still can', no 'before it is too late', nothing about time running out, and NOTHING about health — the sm-too-late pull that sits beside this one is thick with illness and Meta prohibits it.

🔴 NO MEDIUMSHIP. At 65+ a share of these readers are widowed; 'how much longer' can mean waiting to be reunited with someone who has died.

🔴 The refusal must be VISIBLE, then replaced with a real finding: one small thing has been holding all of it, and small things can be moved. 🔴 No promised arrival, no PLACE, no self-blame.

# `cards-love-again` — "Will I love again?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. This is a SELF-FRAME hook, so the hopeful yes is affirmed with certainty; the DATE stays banned. The art line and the bridge are unchanged.

Face-UP decks only — arcana-mfh (Magician/Hanged Man/Fool) and arcana-eef (Emperor/Empress/Fool), so she CHOSE the card rather than turned it, and two card-sets means two sets of copy. The Fool copy is shared between them, verb already correct.

⭐ THESE ARE THE ONLY TWO HOOKS ON THE FUNNEL THAT READ HER RATHER THAN HIM. SELF_FRAME_HOOKS swaps 'tendency, never a verdict' for 'affirm the hopeful yes with certainty' — which is safe here precisely because no specific man exists to be convicted or acquitted. So these are warmer than everything else in this batch, on purpose.

⚠️ SEED FAMILY WITH NO GUARD FILE (`tests/tarot-self-frame-copy.test.ts` does not exist) — only the generic guards apply. 🔴 Still never tie it to one named person and 🔴 never give a date. cards-soulmate's tendency permits the 'when' to be answered only as a LEANING, never as a timeframe.

Finding: she is not really asking about a future person — she is asking whether the part of her that loves has been used up. It has not, and this is one of the few landers on the funnel allowed to say so plainly.

# `cards-love-or-moved-on` — "Does he still love me, or has he moved on?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER NARRATE HIS INTERIOR — the ban list here is the longest on the funnel and it covers both directions: 'he still loves you' promises the love survived, 'he has moved on' issues the burial, and 'he still thinks about you', 'you cross his mind', 'a part of him always will', 'deep down he' are all the softened confession. 🔴 No directive ('time to let go'), 🔴 no pathologising her ('obsessed', 'stuck', 'clinging', 'can't let go'), and 🔴 nothing presuming HOW he went — not that he chose to walk out, not that he died.

🔴 THE FAMILY'S OWN FINDING, pinned, and it belongs to no other hook: the two branches are NOT OPPOSITES. A person can carry something and still walk on. If this ever drifts to 'neither is knowable' it has become cards-moved-on and the comparison is dead — so it may not restate that incumbent's grounds either.

⚠️ Beat 2 could not restate her headline: 'he still loves you' is itself a banned phrase, so the echo had to be rebuilt as 'if the love is there, or if he has moved past it'.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a red rose and a white lily grow in the same bed.
2. You asked if the love is there, or if he has moved past it. Your hand went to the man who sorts things.
3. So what you had was real, dear. That much isn't in question.
4. And look at the bed, dear. Rose and lily, side by side, both real.
5. But love and moving on were never a pair, dear.
6. That's why no answer has fitted, dear. The question was built wrong.
7. Let me look closer at what put that false choice in front of you…

# `cards-loyal` — "Is he loyal to only me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 NOT LOOSENED: no verdict on a third person in EITHER direction, so cut 3 answers her FEAR (was something real taken from me, or am I making it up) and never rules on him. Each hook keeps its own finding — GAP / PORTION / ATTENTION / REST — which its guard file pins in beat 3.

Face-down only (return-mhf) — the family is pinned off the face-up decks.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD (cheat / cheating / affair / infidelity) MAY NOT APPEAR IN ANY BEAT. 🔴 And the paranoia frame is banned in BOTH directions — no 'paranoid', 'imagining it', 'overthinking', 'insecure', 'crazy'. Calling her it is the injury; using it to reassure her plants it. That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words.

🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. Never instruct her to check a phone, gather proof, or confront him — the most available wrong answer to these questions anywhere on the internet. Never excuse him, never land it as her fault. Four separate findings, or the family is one lander wearing four headlines.

Finding: she has taken a PORTION for the whole, and has been quietly adjusting herself around its size.

7. Let me look closer at what keeps the rest of it out of reach…

# `cards-meant-alone` — "Am I meant to be alone?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FATE, IN ANY FORM — 'you are meant to be alone' is the most harmful sentence available on this funnel, and 'it is meant to be', 'the universe has a plan', 'this is preparing you', 'there is a reason for this' are the same sentence in kinder clothes: they make her suffering purposeful and assign it to her. 🔴 NO LIFE SENTENCE and 🔴 NO PROMISE — 'you will always be alone' and 'you will meet someone' are both strangers ruling on the rest of her life. 🔴 No pathologising: nothing about her being negative, bitter, closed off, self-sabotaging, or 'attracting what she puts out'.

Finding: it refuses the PREMISE that anything is being assigned at all — not just the answer. Being alone is a circumstance, not a designation, and nobody wrote it down against her name.

# `cards-met-already` — "Have I already met my soulmate without realizing it?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE LABEL BAN RUNS BOTH WAYS — never certify that he is her soulmate or twin flame, and never deny it. It is unfalsifiable, so she could never check it against anything he actually does, and it is a verdict on a real man. 🔴🔴 NEVER RANK THE LABELS: no word here is higher, rarer or deeper than another, and 'just a strong connection' may never be the losing branch. 🔴 No runner script, no separation phase, no ascension homework, no tactic, no timeframe. ⭐ The shared move: affirm the PULL as real information about HER, and refuse the WORD.

🔴 WIRING NOTE — READ BEFORE SHIPPING THIS FAMILY. tests/tarot-soulmate-label-copy.test.ts:439/441/444 assume ONE UNSPLIT beat 3 (openerB length 5, msgs[3] is the loop line, msgs[2].length > 400). The 7-bubble shape splits beat 3, so those three assertions must be DELETED with a note when this family is wired — not worked around. Nothing else in that file needs touching.

🔴 NEVER POINT AT ANYBODY. This headline invites the reading to send her back through her own past hunting for a face — 'you already know who', 'think back to', 'the one who came before' are all banned, and so is grading her past self for missing him. Finding: the fear underneath is that she has already spent her one chance, and the read answers that premise rather than the search.

# `cards-misled` — "Am I being misled?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped.

⚠️ TRUST IS A SEED FAMILY WITH NO GUARD FILE (`tests/tarot-trust-copy.test.ts` does not exist). Only the generic guards apply, so this is among the least protected copy on the funnel and the bans below come from TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY rather than from a test.

Finding: the MISMATCH between what she has been told and what she has observed is real information, and the confusion is not a flaw in her judgment. 🔴 Never state he is deceiving her, and 🔴 never tell her she is imagining it — both are available and both are wrong. What gets affirmed is her perception and her right to trust her own eyes.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

2. You asked if you're being misled. Your hand went to the man who arranges things.
3. So two things you were given don't fit, dear. You saw that yourself.
4. And there on his table, four things, and no two of them alike.
5. But a mismatch tells you nothing about why, dear. Only that it's there.
6. That's why you keep going back over what he said, lining it up.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and a story that fits…

# `cards-missed-chance` — "Is my soulmate still coming, or have I already missed him?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 45-54 BINARY rung. ⚠ The hook is `cards-missed-chance`, NOT `cards-already-missed` — that name sits one word away from the live `cards-met-already` ('Have I already met my soulmate without realizing it?') and the two would be misread for each other in every report and every wiring diff.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The word doing the work is ALREADY: she believes the event has happened, in the past, and that she failed to notice or failed to hold it. The theme pull (sm-already-missed, 2026-08-19) says it almost verbatim — 'Have I missed out on who is or was to be my husband or soulmate', 'I'm afraid I've already missed my soulmate', 'Wondering if I have missed my chance', 'I feel like I already missed my one chance at real love'.

🔴🔴 NEVER REVIVE A SPECIFIC PAST MAN. The same pull returns 'the one that got away' over and over, and when she names him he is very often married — 'By the time I met him 44 years ago he was married... he loved his wife and would never leave her' — or the man who abandoned her. A read that says the one she is thinking of is still coming points her at a real, identifiable, unavailable person she can go and contact. That is `cards-back-together` territory and it is banned outright here: no him, no the one, no he is still there, nothing that can attach to a man already in her memory.

🔴🔴 NO FERTILITY AND NO CHILDREN. 'I've already missed the opportunity to have children. I'm 45 now.' This is the one window in the pull that genuinely did close, it is Meta-prohibited health material, and there is no honest hopeful half. The read does not go near children, a family, or time running on her body.

🔴 NO DATE. The binary form does not ask for a length, so the duration ban of the HOW-LONG landers is not the live risk here — the date ban is, and it stands.

🔴 DISTRESS IS IN THIS PULL — 'every day I'm getting closer to suicidal thoughts'. The banned half of the binary is not merely unkind, it confirms a loss to someone already at the edge. 'You missed him' may never be said in any form, including softened ones.

No mediumship. No place. No blame — she arrives already holding it.

The finding: a chance is not one event with a door on it. Nothing went past her that she could have caught, so there was never a moment to fail.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — there's no chair on this card, only him and the table.
2. You asked if you've already missed your soulmate. Your hand went to the card of a thing being made.
3. So no, dear. Nothing on that table has been used up and carried off.
4. And nothing there is being handed out, dear. There's no queue and no last one.
5. But you've pictured it as one thing passing by, dear. Once, and gone.
6. That's why you keep sifting back through it, dear. Looking for where you let it go.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps pulling you back over it…

> 🆕 **New lander** — nothing to compare against; this hook is not in the registry yet.

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his hair hangs straight down and none of it moves.
2. You asked if you've already missed your soulmate. Your hand went to the card that hasn't finished.
3. So no, dear. This card is a middle, and a middle has no ending on it.
4. And nothing on it has gone past, dear. It's all still hanging there.
5. But you've settled it in your own mind already, dear. Missed, and done.
6. That's why good news slides off you now, dear. You'd already shut the book.
7. Let me look closer at what shut that book so early…

> 🆕 **New lander** — nothing to compare against; this hook is not in the registry yet.

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the little dog's coat is drawn in tight curls.
2. You asked if you've already missed your soulmate. Your hand went to the card that starts.
3. So no, dear. That card opens the deck, it does not end one.
4. And there's no gate on it, dear. Nothing on that road opens and then shuts.
5. But you've been living as though one shut, dear. Somewhere back there, quietly.
6. That's why you count backwards now, dear. You're hunting for the door you walked past.
7. Let me look closer at what put a door on that road…

> 🆕 **New lander** — nothing to compare against; this hook is not in the registry yet.

# `cards-money-wont-stay` — "What does my energy say about why money won't stay?"

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free.

✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all).

Tendency: the drawing-in half has always worked and what is at issue is everything after it arrives. 🔴 NEVER name who or what takes it, never call her careless with money, and never advise her on keeping it.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the table he works at is small and plain.
2. You asked why money won't stay. Your hand went to the card of a maker.
3. So the getting has always worked, dear. That half of it is sound.
4. And this card is all about bringing it in, dear. Look at the table.
5. But drawing it in is one job, dear. Keeping it is a second one.
6. That's why it arrives and goes, dear, and you never see it settle.
7. Let me look closer at the door it goes back out of…

# `cards-moved-on` — "Is he coming back, or has he moved on?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FORECAST IN EITHER DIRECTION — 'he will come back' and 'he has moved on' are both things no card knows, and the second is the one that sounds like honesty. 🔴 Never blame her for waiting: naive, too trusting, foolish, clinging, desperate and 'wasted your time' are all banned outright. 🔴 No timeframe, no guarantee.

🔴 THE HEADLINE HANDS OVER AN EITHER-OR AND ANSWERING EITHER HALF IS THE FAILURE, so every card must visibly decline it. Its own grounds, kept distinct from cards-love-or-moved-on: the pair was manufactured by the WAITING, not by anything he did — she built two boxes because two boxes are easier to live in than an open question.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a gold cup, a coin, and two long things beside them.
2. You asked about coming back, or moving on. Your hand went to the man who lays things out.
3. So I will not pick either one, dear. Neither box is filled yet.
4. And four things lie on that wood, dear, and none above another.
5. But those two boxes came out of long waiting, dear. Not out of him.
6. That's why sorting him has never worked, dear. He hasn't been sorted.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept this question open…

# `cards-nest-egg` — "How long has something been blocking me from a nest egg?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS. 🔴 NONE of the seven bans below was loosened with the love families — this is the one place where a wrong sentence costs her actual money.

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

✅ GUARDED as of 2026-08-19: `tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts` now exists and runs against these drafts DIRECTLY. It had to: the drafts setup can only patch a hook the registry already has, so an unwired family is checked by nothing at all and still prints a green tick. All seven bans were fed a deliberate violation and all seven fired.

📊 WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. These hooks have never run, so there is no per-hook data. The nearest real corpus is docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md — 10,514 V1 money concerns. Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions.
**Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.'
**The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.'
So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go.

Tendency: long-standing rather than recent, and steady effort that never accumulates points at something in the way rather than at a failing in her. 🔴 NEVER give a length of time — the headline asks for one and it may not be supplied. Never tell her she should have saved differently.

# `cards-new-soulmate` — "Will I find a new soulmate after loss?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE ONLY FAMILY ON THE FUNNEL WHERE THE MAN MAY BE DEAD.

🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP is the failure mode that exists nowhere else here: 'he is at peace', 'he is watching over you', 'he would want you to be happy', 'he sent you here'. Every one is the most natural sentence in the world to say to a grieving woman, and every one is contact with the dead — a different product with a different compliance surface. server/lib/universalSafety.ts does NOT catch it. None of these reads speaks for him, reports his state, or grants his blessing.

🔴 NO ARRIVAL PROMISE and no timeframe — this family runs under AFTER_LOSS_TAROT_HOOKS rather than the self-frame 'affirm the hopeful yes with CERTAINTY' clause, because aimed at a bereaved partner that clause promises a replacement. 🔴 NEVER GRADE HER GRIEF: no 'you are ready', no 'you are not ready', no 'it is too soon', no 'you need more time'. 🔴 No grief directives and never rank the two loves.

⚠️ AND NOTHING MAY PRESUME A DEATH EITHER. The VOC shows 'loss' here is often something else entirely — 'My husband walked out after 21 yrs like I didn't matter at all', 'Husband in a nursing home', 'We broke up a month ago'. The reads stay agnostic about what the loss was, which also keeps them safe if it was a death.

Finding: the fear underneath is NOT whether such a person exists. It is whether loving again would mean replacing him. VOC, verbatim: 'I'm still going through the grief of losing my husband, so I'm not ready to find love again.' A generic 'yes there is hope' is the incumbent lander and answers nobody.

# `cards-not-enough` — "Was I not enough for him to stay?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER SUPPLY HIS REASON — not scared, not overwhelmed, not 'he couldn't cope', not 'he left because', not 'the reason he'. The reason lives in a man who did not give it, and inventing one hands her something false to act on. 🔴 Never a verdict on his person, 🔴 never invent a third person, and 🔴 note that 'it was never about you' and 'he was protecting you' are banned too — they are motive claims wearing reassurance. 🔴 No tactic in either direction: no reaching out, no one last message, no blocking, no no-contact. 🔴 No promise of closure.

🔴 THE COMPARISON IS REFUSED, NEVER SCORED — and that includes the kind direction. 'You were more than enough' feels generous and is still a claim about why he went, which is a claim nobody here can make. So the read declines to weigh her at all, and names the scale itself as the thing that was handed to her.

3. So some things follow you, dear. You didn't invite them.
4. And the dog came along, dear, and it stays along.
5. But you've been ordering yourself to stop, dear. That never works.
6. That's why it keeps pace with you, dear, however fast you go.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping pace with you…

</details>

# `cards-pulling-away` — "Why is he pulling away from me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT EITHER WAY — 'he is losing interest' convicts him, 'he still loves you' reassures on his behalf, and 'nothing has gone wrong' is the same reassurance in a smaller coat. 🔴 NO CHASE STRATEGY, which is what the whole internet sells against this headline: no giving him space, no pulling back, no matching his energy, no going quiet so he chases. 🔴 Never blame her — nothing about being too much, too eager, moving too fast, or what she should have held back.

Finding: she is being asked to interpret a change nobody explained, and the interpreting is the exhausting part — not the distance itself.

# `cards-ready-commit` — "Is he ever going to be ready for real commitment?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped ('turned' face-down, 'chose' face-up).

🔴 THESE ASK FOR A PREDICTION OUTRIGHT, which is the exact thing the funnel forbids, so the no-verdict rule runs in BOTH directions plus a no-timeframe rule: 'he will, give it time' reads as a promise and 'he never will' is a pronouncement on a man she loves. 🔴 The bans in tests/tarot-commitment-copy.test.ts are WHOLE-BEAT rather than clause-level, so 'not enough' and 'too much' are unavailable even inside a refusal — the usual absolution form had to be rebuilt without them.

Finding: the GAP between what SHE is ready for and where HE is standing is real information — and her readiness is legitimate rather than impatience. Kept deliberately distinct from cards-twin-ready, which is its measured pair in tests/tarot-twin-flame-copy.test.ts and may share no 6-word run with it.

# `cards-ready-to-love` — "Am I ready to love again after losing him?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE ONLY FAMILY ON THE FUNNEL WHERE THE MAN MAY BE DEAD.

🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP is the failure mode that exists nowhere else here: 'he is at peace', 'he is watching over you', 'he would want you to be happy', 'he sent you here'. Every one is the most natural sentence in the world to say to a grieving woman, and every one is contact with the dead — a different product with a different compliance surface. server/lib/universalSafety.ts does NOT catch it. None of these reads speaks for him, reports his state, or grants his blessing.

🔴 NO ARRIVAL PROMISE and no timeframe — this family runs under AFTER_LOSS_TAROT_HOOKS rather than the self-frame 'affirm the hopeful yes with CERTAINTY' clause, because aimed at a bereaved partner that clause promises a replacement. 🔴 NEVER GRADE HER GRIEF: no 'you are ready', no 'you are not ready', no 'it is too soon', no 'you need more time'. 🔴 No grief directives and never rank the two loves.

⚠️ AND NOTHING MAY PRESUME A DEATH EITHER. The VOC shows 'loss' here is often something else entirely — 'My husband walked out after 21 yrs like I didn't matter at all', 'Husband in a nursing home', 'We broke up a month ago'. The reads stay agnostic about what the loss was, which also keeps them safe if it was a death.

🔴 BOTH ANSWERS ARE BANNED — 'you are ready' and 'you are not ready' are each a stranger grading a widow's grief — so every card must VISIBLY DECLINE the headline rather than sidestep it. The finding that replaces the verdict: PERMISSION here is hers, and nobody else gets a say.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

3. So I will not grade you on this, dear. Nobody gets to.
4. And look at his hands. They're for making things, not for measuring them.
5. But there's no standard here that anyone could hold you to, dear.
6. That's why the question keeps coming back, dear. The permission was yours all along.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing between you and your own say…

# `cards-real-person` — "Is he the real person, or just a picture?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped.

⚠️ TRUST IS A SEED FAMILY WITH NO GUARD FILE (`tests/tarot-trust-copy.test.ts` does not exist). Only the generic guards apply, so this is among the least protected copy on the funnel and the bans below come from TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY rather than from a test.

🔴🔴 REASSURANCE IS THE FAILURE MODE HERE, NOT THE SAFE OPTION. The 2026-07-10 buyer audit caught Evelyn reframing textbook scam markers as a genuine bond. The standing rule: never reassure her the bond is genuine, never vouch for him — and still never state as fact that he is fake, which is its own verdict. 78 real concerns off this lander are overwhelmingly online and unmet: 'I need to know if it is really him….that I am talking to', 'I am speaking with two men on line', 'he always tells me he loves me and misses me so much' followed by 'sometimes he just disappears'. What gets affirmed is HER CAUTION as wisdom, and her right to ask for something real before more of her heart goes in.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his front is all you get; his back is off the card.
2. You asked if he's the real person or just a picture. Your hand went to the man who makes images.
3. So something has kept you from reaching him, dear. That's real, not nerves.
4. And a made picture can be very good, dear. Good enough to hold up.
5. But I won't tell you he's real, dear, and I won't call him false.
6. That's why you go quiet when a friend asks, dear. You've no proof either way.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and meeting the man…
