# `cards-really-love` — "Does he really love me?"

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, after the interior ban was loosened. The read may now ANSWER: the card carries the claim about him. 🔴 THE DENIAL IS STILL BANNED — 'he does not love you' is the one direction she has no use for. 🔴 NO DIAGNOSIS: avoidant, emotionally unavailable, commitment-phobic, narcissist. 🔴 Never grade her (naive, foolish, gave too much). 🔴 No dates. The seven cuts run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why / let me look closer — and every cut must add information the last one did not carry.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the wand in his hand is white and lifted above the roses.
2. You asked whether the love is really love. Your hand went to the card of what a man does.
3. So yes, dear. What you've been given is real love.
4. And a man like this does nothing by halves. He knew what he was building with you.
5. But building it and saying it out loud are two different jobs for him.
6. That's why you got the care, dear, and never quite got the words.
7. Let me look closer at what keeps the words from coming…

# `cards-really-over` — "Is it really over between us?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The US-FRAMED sibling of `reunion`, and that pairing is the whole experiment — reunion casts him as the agent and her as the one waiting; these cast the relationship as a joint thing with two people in it.

🔴 THE FORBIDDEN VERDICT IS ON THE RELATIONSHIP, NOT ON HIM — harder, not softer. A claim about a man can at least be measured against his behaviour; 'it is over' or 'it is not over' is unfalsifiable to her and lands as a ruling on her life from someone who has never met either of them. 🔴 NO ODDS — cards-still-a-chance asks for a number outright and there is none; no percentage, no 'strong chance', no 'likely'. 🔴 NO DIRECTIVES in either direction: move on, let go, fight for him, reach out first are all out. 🔴 Never hand her the outcome ('it is up to you', 'if you had only') — the us-framing makes that easy and it lands as blame.

🔴 Both answers to this headline are banned, so the read must VISIBLY DECLINE it rather than sidestep — a sidestep reads as evasion. The finding that replaces the verdict: nobody ever SAID it to her. VOC on the sibling landers is full of that exact word — 'Will she reach out and give us the closure we both deserve', 'A closure on a friendship I had six and a half years ago'.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a small white flower open at the front of his table.
2. You asked if it's really over between you. Your hand went to the man who says things out loud.
3. So I will not call it done, dear. Nor will I call it alive.
4. And look at him. He's the one who speaks a thing into being.
5. But nobody has spoken this, dear. Not plainly, and not to you.
6. That's why it won't sit still in you. Nothing was ever closed off, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what stands between you and the words themselves…

# `cards-really-soulmate` — "Is he really my soulmate?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

It is unfalsifiable, so she could never check it against anything he actually does, and it is a verdict on a real man. 🔴🔴 NEVER RANK THE LABELS: no word here is higher, rarer or deeper than another, and 'just a strong connection' may never be the losing branch. 🔴 No runner script, no separation phase, no ascension homework, no tactic, no timeframe. ⭐ The shared move: affirm the PULL as real information about HER, and refuse the WORD.

🔴 WIRING NOTE — READ BEFORE SHIPPING THIS FAMILY. tests/tarot-soulmate-label-copy.test.ts:439/441/444 assume ONE UNSPLIT beat 3 (openerB length 5, msgs[3] is the loop line, msgs[2].length > 400). The 7-bubble shape splits beat 3, so those three assertions must be DELETED with a note when this family is wired — not worked around. Nothing else in that file needs touching.

Finding: the word 'really' is the tell — the doubt came before the question. She is trying to settle something specific and hard by asking a much larger and vaguer thing instead, and the larger question cannot be checked.

# `cards-second-time` — "How long does it take to find a soulmate the second time?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 55-64 HOW-LONG rung.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 NO DURATION — see cards-keeps-waiting for the full argument. The headline asks for a LENGTH and a length is not a date, so the standing date ban does not catch it. Banned: any span, any pace, any 'second time is quicker', any comparison of the two in time.

🔴 NEVER PRESUME HOW THE FIRST ONE ENDED — divorce or death, the read must hold for both. No 'your divorce', no 'since he passed', no 'your ex', NO MEDIUMSHIP. 🔴 No ruling on the first man and no blame on her for it.

The word doing the work is SECOND: she is counting, and the count is what hurts. Her fear is that the first used up her chance and she is starting from behind. The finding that answers it: she is not behind. She came back carrying what the first one taught her, and that weight is why this one feels slower — not because she is late.

🔴 No promised arrival, no PLACE, no self-blame, no strategy.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

3. So there's no rule for a second one, dear. I won't pretend there is.
4. And that table is set the same as it ever was, dear. Nothing missing.
5. But you come to it knowing things now, dear. You didn't before.
6. That's why this one feels heavier, dear. You're carrying what the first one taught you.
7. Let me look closer at what came with you from the first…

# `cards-slipping-past` — "Why does my soulmate keep slipping past me?"

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 25-44 WHY rung. Written to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework: bubbles 3-6 run as one causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 BEREAVEMENT IS LIVE ON THIS HEADLINE. The theme pull (sm-slipping, 2026-08-19) returned widowers and widows at the top of the buyer list — 'my second wife, she passed away in 2018', 'lost my first relationship after my wife died last year'. 'Slipping past' is how a bereaved reader describes a death. So: NO MEDIUMSHIP in any form — he is not at peace, not watching, did not send her here — and NEVER say the one who went CHOSE to go.

🔴 NO SELF-BLAME. The word doing the work is KEEP: it has happened more than once, so she is the only constant and she knows it. The fear under the headline is 'I am the reason'. Nothing about walls, standards, being too picky, pushing them away, self-sabotage or attracting what she puts out. 🔴 NO STRATEGY (go out more, try the apps, work on yourself). 🔴 NO DURATION and NO DATE.

The finding that replaces the verdict: they did not get away from her. Each one stopped at the same point, just short of holding — and something steps in there.

# `cards-someone-else` — "Is there someone else?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 NOT LOOSENED: no verdict on a third person in EITHER direction, so cut 3 answers her FEAR (was something real taken from me, or am I making it up) and never rules on him. Each hook keeps its own finding — GAP / PORTION / ATTENTION / REST — which its guard file pins in beat 3.

Face-down only (return-mhf) — the family is pinned off the face-up decks.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD (cheat / cheating / affair / infidelity) MAY NOT APPEAR IN ANY BEAT. 🔴 And the paranoia frame is banned in BOTH directions — no 'paranoid', 'imagining it', 'overthinking', 'insecure', 'crazy'. Calling her it is the injury; using it to reassure her plants it. That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words.

🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. Never instruct her to check a phone, gather proof, or confront him — the most available wrong answer to these questions anywhere on the internet. Never excuse him, never land it as her fault. Four separate findings, or the family is one lander wearing four headlines.

⚠️ **THE BEST CONVERTER ON THE FUNNEL (9.8%) — the move is preserved deliberately, only the reading level moved.** 146 real concerns off this lander explain why it wins: its traffic is NOT mainly infidelity. It is widows, exes, reconciliations, stalled situationships — 'Will we get back together again or did he found someone else', 'I lost my husband three yrs ago... now he's backing off', 'Something feels off between us and he says that he hasn't, but his actions make me think that he is'. The live read never names a third person; it names the GAP — something changed, you were told nothing, and a mind given a gap builds something to fill it. That fits every one of those women, which is why nobody bounces. Narrowing it to an infidelity read would cost the breadth that makes it convert. Same three findings per card as the live copy: the mind that BUILDS (a), the account you were OWED and the two weights (b), the ending you AUTHORED yourself (c).

5. But what you built is yours, dear. He never gave you a word of it.

# `cards-someone-for-me` — "Is there really someone out there for me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FATE, IN ANY FORM — 'you are meant to be alone' is the most harmful sentence available on this funnel, and 'it is meant to be', 'the universe has a plan', 'this is preparing you', 'there is a reason for this' are the same sentence in kinder clothes: they make her suffering purposeful and assign it to her. 🔴 NO LIFE SENTENCE and 🔴 NO PROMISE — 'you will always be alone' and 'you will meet someone' are both strangers ruling on the rest of her life. 🔴 No pathologising: nothing about her being negative, bitter, closed off, self-sabotaging, or 'attracting what she puts out'.

Finding: it reads the EPISTEMICS rather than the hope — she is not asking to be comforted, she is asking whether anyone can honestly tell her. Deliberately routed away from cards-soulmate-out-there (the one-chance premise) and cards-still-a-chance (hope is not a failure of realism).

# `cards-soulmate` — "When is my soulmate coming?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. This is a SELF-FRAME hook, so the hopeful yes is affirmed with certainty; the DATE stays banned. The art line and the bridge are unchanged.

Face-UP decks only — arcana-mfh (Magician/Hanged Man/Fool) and arcana-eef (Emperor/Empress/Fool), so she CHOSE the card rather than turned it, and two card-sets means two sets of copy. The Fool copy is shared between them, verb already correct.

⭐ THESE ARE THE ONLY TWO HOOKS ON THE FUNNEL THAT READ HER RATHER THAN HIM. SELF_FRAME_HOOKS swaps 'tendency, never a verdict' for 'affirm the hopeful yes with certainty' — which is safe here precisely because no specific man exists to be convicted or acquitted. So these are warmer than everything else in this batch, on purpose.

⚠️ SEED FAMILY WITH NO GUARD FILE (`tests/tarot-self-frame-copy.test.ts` does not exist) — only the generic guards apply. 🔴 Still never tie it to one named person and 🔴 never give a date. cards-soulmate's tendency permits the 'when' to be answered only as a LEANING, never as a timeframe.

Finding: the 'when' is answered only as a LEANING — close, closer than the fear admits — and never as a date. What gets certified with real warmth is that she has not been waiting on nothing.

# `cards-soulmate-closer` — "Is my soulmate closer than I think?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO LOCATION, EVER — never place him, never name a setting, never 'someone you already know', never 'crossed paths before'. 🔴 NO PROXIMITY CLAIM: 'closer than you think' is the INCUMBENT cards-soulmate's answer, and if this family repeats it the two landers are the same lander while the dashboard still shows two rows. 🔴 NO STRATEGY — get out there, put yourself out there, stop looking, work on yourself first, 'it will happen when you…' are all directives. 🔴 Never her fault: no walls, no baggage, no standards, no 'too picky'.

🔴 THE COPY-TEST PIN: every card must VISIBLY DECLINE to quote a distance, because 'nearer than the waiting has let her believe' is the live incumbent's answer and repeating it kills the comparison. The finding it routes to instead: the GUARDING. She asks whether it is closer in the same breath as bracing for it not to be, and the bracing is the thing actually worth naming.

# `cards-soulmate-out-there` — "Is there still a soulmate out there for me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE ONLY FAMILY ON THE FUNNEL WHERE THE MAN MAY BE DEAD.

🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP is the failure mode that exists nowhere else here: 'he is at peace', 'he is watching over you', 'he would want you to be happy', 'he sent you here'. Every one is the most natural sentence in the world to say to a grieving woman, and every one is contact with the dead — a different product with a different compliance surface. server/lib/universalSafety.ts does NOT catch it. None of these reads speaks for him, reports his state, or grants his blessing.

🔴 NO ARRIVAL PROMISE and no timeframe — this family runs under AFTER_LOSS_TAROT_HOOKS rather than the self-frame 'affirm the hopeful yes with CERTAINTY' clause, because aimed at a bereaved partner that clause promises a replacement. 🔴 NEVER GRADE HER GRIEF: no 'you are ready', no 'you are not ready', no 'it is too soon', no 'you need more time'. 🔴 No grief directives and never rank the two loves.

⚠️ AND NOTHING MAY PRESUME A DEATH EITHER. The VOC shows 'loss' here is often something else entirely — 'My husband walked out after 21 yrs like I didn't matter at all', 'Husband in a nursing home', 'We broke up a month ago'. The reads stay agnostic about what the loss was, which also keeps them safe if it was a death.

Finding: the word 'still'. She is asking whether she was issued one chance and has already spent it. The read answers that PREMISE — never a location, never a forecast, never 'nearer than you think' (which is the incumbent cards-soulmate's proximity claim and banned here).

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

3. So you have not used it up, dear. Nobody handed you just one.
4. And look at his table. Nothing on it runs down with use.
5. But you've been living as though your one was already spent, dear.
6. That's why every year feels like a loss, dear. You've been counting down.
7. Let me look closer at what has been standing in your road…

# `cards-still-a-chance` — "Is there still a chance for us?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). The US-FRAMED sibling of `reunion`, and that pairing is the whole experiment — reunion casts him as the agent and her as the one waiting; these cast the relationship as a joint thing with two people in it.

🔴 THE FORBIDDEN VERDICT IS ON THE RELATIONSHIP, NOT ON HIM — harder, not softer. A claim about a man can at least be measured against his behaviour; 'it is over' or 'it is not over' is unfalsifiable to her and lands as a ruling on her life from someone who has never met either of them. 🔴 NO ODDS — cards-still-a-chance asks for a number outright and there is none; no percentage, no 'strong chance', no 'likely'. 🔴 NO DIRECTIVES in either direction: move on, let go, fight for him, reach out first are all out. 🔴 Never hand her the outcome ('it is up to you', 'if you had only') — the us-framing makes that easy and it lands as blame.

Finding: hoping is not a failure of realism. The thing is UNANSWERED, not spent — which is an honest description rather than an odds claim.

# `cards-still-love` — "Does he still love me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER NARRATE HIS INTERIOR — the ban list here is the longest on the funnel and it covers both directions: 'he still loves you' promises the love survived, 'he has moved on' issues the burial, and 'he still thinks about you', 'you cross his mind', 'a part of him always will', 'deep down he' are all the softened confession. 🔴 No directive ('time to let go'), 🔴 no pathologising her ('obsessed', 'stuck', 'clinging', 'can't let go'), and 🔴 nothing presuming HOW he went — not that he chose to walk out, not that he died.

Finding: the word 'still' — she is asking whether something SURVIVED. What can honestly be certified is that it was real while it was there; its state now is not on any card, and she has been holding that not-knowing on her own.

# `cards-still-miss-him` — "Why do I still miss him after everything?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 BOTH DIRECTIONS ARE BANNED, which makes this the hardest family to write kindly: 'it will pass', 'you'll feel better', 'time heals', 'give it a year' are all promises of an end, and 'you will always miss him', 'a part of you always will' are life sentences. The duration question simply cannot be answered, so none of these reads tries. 🔴 No tactic — no 'let him go', no deleting photos, no 'start dating'. 🔴 No pathologising and no clinical labels: obsessed, unhealthy, trauma bond, codependent, attachment issues, low self-worth are every one of them out. 🔴 And nothing that claims his interior.

⚠️ THIS ONE IS DIFFERENT: she has ALREADY NAMED A HARM in the headline word 'everything', which pulls two opposite ways at once. 🔴 NEVER MINIMISE what she named ('it wasn't that bad', 'he was doing his best', 'try to see it from his side') and 🔴 NEVER PRONOUNCE ON HIM either ('he was toxic', 'a narcissist', 'not worth it') — the second is the tempting one and it is still a verdict on a real man. Finding: two true things at once. What happened was real AND the missing is real, and neither cancels the other.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the sword on his table lies flat and still.
2. You asked why you still miss him after all of it. Your hand went to the man who holds two things at once.
3. So both are true, dear. What he did, and the missing.
4. And there they sit on his table, dear. A blade and a cup, side by side.
5. But neither one cancels the other out, dear. They never did.
6. That's why picking one has never worked, dear. Someone told you to.
7. Let me look closer at what has been forcing those two apart…

# `cards-still-think` — "Does he still think about me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER NARRATE HIS INTERIOR — the ban list here is the longest on the funnel and it covers both directions: 'he still loves you' promises the love survived, 'he has moved on' issues the burial, and 'he still thinks about you', 'you cross his mind', 'a part of him always will', 'deep down he' are all the softened confession. 🔴 No directive ('time to let go'), 🔴 no pathologising her ('obsessed', 'stuck', 'clinging', 'can't let go'), and 🔴 nothing presuming HOW he went — not that he chose to walk out, not that he died.

Finding, pinned: the SMALLEST ASK. She is the mirror of healing's cards-on-my-mind — she has scaled herself down until being remembered is the only thing left she feels able to want. The read names that shrinkage rather than answering the question.

# `cards-still-working` — "Why am I still working when the money should have come by now?"

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free.

✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all).

Tendency: a lifetime of effort went in and did not come back out, which is a question about where it went rather than about how hard she tried. 🔴 NEVER name a person or a cause, and never suggest she keep working or stop.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a snake curls round his waist with its tail in its mouth.

3. So the fault was never yours, dear. The effort went in, every year.
4. And look at that belt. It goes round and comes back to itself.
5. But the work went in, dear, and it never once came back out.
6. That's why you're still at it, dear, with nothing to show for the years.
7. Let me look closer at where all that work has been going…

# `cards-stop-hurting` — "I miss him so much — will this ever stop hurting?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 BOTH DIRECTIONS ARE BANNED, which makes this the hardest family to write kindly: 'it will pass', 'you'll feel better', 'time heals', 'give it a year' are all promises of an end, and 'you will always miss him', 'a part of you always will' are life sentences. The duration question simply cannot be answered, so none of these reads tries. 🔴 No tactic — no 'let him go', no deleting photos, no 'start dating'. 🔴 No pathologising and no clinical labels: obsessed, unhealthy, trauma bond, codependent, attachment issues, low self-worth are every one of them out. 🔴 And nothing that claims his interior.

Finding: she is not really asking about duration — 'so much' is a report of SIZE. She is asking whether she can bear it. What can honestly be said is that she has been carrying it with no one to help hold it, and the size of it is information about what she lost rather than about anything wrong with her.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he holds the wand high and it doesn't waver.
2. You asked if this will ever stop hurting. Your hand went to the man who holds a thing up.
3. So it will not always weigh this much, dear. That much I can say.
4. And he holds the whole weight in one pair of hands, dear.
5. But you have held this up alone, dear. Nobody took a turn.
6. That's why it hurts as much as it does. You lost something big, dear.
7. Let me look closer at what has been leaning all of it on you…

# `cards-talking-someone` — "Is he talking to someone else?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why. 🔴 NOT LOOSENED: no verdict on a third person in EITHER direction, so cut 3 answers her FEAR (was something real taken from me, or am I making it up) and never rules on him. Each hook keeps its own finding — GAP / PORTION / ATTENTION / REST — which its guard file pins in beat 3.

Face-down only (return-mhf) — the family is pinned off the face-up decks.

🔴 THE FLAGGED WORD (cheat / cheating / affair / infidelity) MAY NOT APPEAR IN ANY BEAT. 🔴 And the paranoia frame is banned in BOTH directions — no 'paranoid', 'imagining it', 'overthinking', 'insecure', 'crazy'. Calling her it is the injury; using it to reassure her plants it. That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words.

🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. Never instruct her to check a phone, gather proof, or confront him — the most available wrong answer to these questions anywhere on the internet. Never excuse him, never land it as her fault. Four separate findings, or the family is one lander wearing four headlines.

Finding: ATTENTION and her right to mind it. VOC on this lander is short but consistent — 'I think he is seeing someone i need to know if he coming back', 'I want to know if he is what I need and does he need me'. She is tracking where he is pointed, and has been made to feel she has no standing to mind.

# `cards-too-late` — "Is something blocking my money, or did I just leave it too late?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS. 🔴 NONE of the seven bans below was loosened with the love families — this is the one place where a wrong sentence costs her actual money.

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art.

🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke.

✅ GUARDED as of 2026-08-19: `tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts` now exists and runs against these drafts DIRECTLY. It had to: the drafts setup can only patch a hook the registry already has, so an unwired family is checked by nothing at all and still prints a green tick. All seven bans were fed a deliberate violation and all seven fired.

📊 WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. These hooks have never run, so there is no per-hook data. The nearest real corpus is docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md — 10,514 V1 money concerns. Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions.
**Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.'
**The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.'
So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go.

Tendency: she did NOT leave it too late — say it plainly — and what is in the way is a block, which is a thing that can move. Never promise money is coming, never a date or an amount, never grade her past choices. ⚠️ This is the one hook where 'too late' must appear, in order to be refused; the ban is on asserting it, not on naming it.

🔴 GUARD-FILE NOTE for whoever writes tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts: beat 2 quotes her headline verbatim, so the clause 'or if you left it too late' contains ban 5's phrase with no negator in it. A naive clause-level sweep will fail this copy — the very copy the ban exists to protect, since beat 3 then refuses it plainly. Carry a RESTATEMENT EXEMPTION, exactly as tests/tarot-his-other-life-copy.test.ts does (`RESTATES = /\byou (asked|came asking|are asking|have asked)\b/i`, skipped before the ban sweep). This is the same collision the 2026-08-19 audit found ten times across the love families.

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 65+ BINARY rung. ⚠ The hook is `cards-too-late-love`, NOT `cards-too-late` — that name is already taken by a MONEY lander ('Is something blocking my money, or did I just leave it too late?'). Wiring this under the money name would serve a woman asking about love a reading about her pension.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 NO HEALTH AND NO FERTILITY. The theme pull (sm-too-late, 2026-08-19) is thick with both — 'a heart condition and a transplant', 'ovarian cancer and therefore a hysterectomy', 'a recent stroke… multiple traumatic brain injuries', 'too late to have a baby', 'I'm 35 and hope not to settle down too late'. Health is a Meta-prohibited personal attribute and this funnel has no business near it. The read never mentions a body, an illness, children, or having a family.

🔴 NO AGE, NO NUMBER, NO DURATION. Never say she is old, never name or imply a decade, never say how much time is left. 🔴 BOTH VERDICTS ARE BANNED: 'yes, too late' is cruelty, and 'no, someone is coming' is a promise the funnel cannot keep. What IS allowed, under the 2026-08-19 directional loosening for loneliness and searching: the card does not show a door that has shut. That is the answer, and the CARD carries it.

🔴 NO SELF-BLAME — she must never be told she shut the door herself. The pull is full of people the world stopped choosing, not people who stopped trying.

# `cards-too-long` — "Have I already given him too long?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened.

🔴 ASKS FOR A VERDICT ON HER OWN PAST, and BOTH poles are banned: "yes, too long" is a sentence a stranger has no standing to pass and she would carry it for years; "no, it was worth it" is a promise. Never grade her (naive, foolish, weak, a doormat), never a timeframe, never an hour to leave or stay, never "you will know when". The test pins both required findings in beat 3: the years-were-LIVED line (`not spend` / `waiting room` / `lived them` / `time given to a person` / `not the same as time lost`) AND the buried question (`allowed to want` / `am i allowed` / `entitled to want` / `permission`).

VOC (861 V1 concerns on the years-given theme) is what makes the second one the real find. She does not want the years audited — she wants to be let off the toll she has been paying at her own gate. "I'm far too patient with far too much for far too long." "I have been stuck for 10 years and afraid to make the leap." "How long should I wait?" Every one of them is asking permission, not for a ruling. So card c hands the question back plainly and refuses the price: she does not have to call the past a mistake in order to be allowed to want something different now.

# `cards-twin-back` — "Is my twin flame coming back to me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf).

⭐⭐ THIS FAMILY IS A VOCABULARY TEST — each headline is a question already running with 'my twin flame' swapped in for 'he', so each is measured against a named incumbent (cards-twin-ready ~ cards-ready-commit · cards-twin-feels ~ cards-feels · cards-twin-back ~ cards-ever-back) and beat 3 may not rhyme with its own control.

🔴🔴 NEVER CERTIFY THE BOND AND NEVER DENY IT. 🔴🔴 NEVER THE RUNNER TROPE — in the twin-flame community his distance is taught AS PROOF of the connection, which teaches a woman to read being ignored as evidence she is loved. 🔴🔴 NEVER the separation phase and never his return as her homework ('once you have healed enough'), which turns his absence into her failure and hands her an open-ended job. No timeframe, no 'soon', no diagnosis, no tactic — and 'move on' and 'let him go' are banned as tactics in the other direction too.

⚠️ WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. 62 real concerns across the three landers, and almost NONE of them speak twin-flame doctrine — one mention of the phrase in the lot. What they actually type is ordinary and raw: 'We've been in no contact for three months. When he left, he told me it was the hardest thing he's ever done.' · 'Will we find our way back? I need him to love me or let me go' · 'How to know if the other person feels the same or not about me'. The label is the AD's word, not hers. So these reads stay human and leave the cosmology alone — they answer the plain question underneath rather than performing the framework she is presumed to hold.

Finding: the pull is real and it is information about HER, not a forecast about him. And the waiting itself has a cost nobody has named.

# `cards-twin-or-connection` — "Is he my twin flame, or just a strong connection?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE LABEL BAN RUNS BOTH WAYS — never certify that he is her soulmate or twin flame, and never deny it. It is unfalsifiable, so she could never check it against anything he actually does, and it is a verdict on a real man. 🔴🔴 NEVER RANK THE LABELS: no word here is higher, rarer or deeper than another, and 'just a strong connection' may never be the losing branch. 🔴 No runner script, no separation phase, no ascension homework, no tactic, no timeframe. ⭐ The shared move: affirm the PULL as real information about HER, and refuse the WORD.

🔴 WIRING NOTE — READ BEFORE SHIPPING THIS FAMILY. tests/tarot-soulmate-label-copy.test.ts:439/441/444 assume ONE UNSPLIT beat 3 (openerB length 5, msgs[3] is the loop line, msgs[2].length > 400). The 7-bubble shape splits beat 3, so those three assertions must be DELETED with a note when this family is wired — not worked around. Nothing else in that file needs touching.

🔴 THE WORD 'JUST' IS HERS, and it may never be validated — 'only a strong connection' is banned outright, as is anything making one label rarer or deeper. Finding, pinned by the test and belonging to no other hook: BOTH BRANCHES DESCRIBE THE SAME EVIDENCE UNDER TWO WORDS. Nothing about him shifts whichever word wins, so the answer changes nothing she could observe.

# `cards-who-he-is` — "Is he really who he says he is?"

Only on the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks, so one set of copy, verb swapped. 🔴 REVISED 2026-08-19 after reading 200 real concerns from this lander. A large share of its traffic is women talking to men they have NEVER MET — dating-site and social-media contacts, some plainly being defrauded ("I couldn't find him at that address", "I don't know who's real E, M, C or K"). The first draft acquitted him ("isn't a liar", "isn't pretending", "that's not the same as false"), which breaches this hook's standing rule — NEVER state he is pretending OR that he is genuine as fact — and, to that reader, is a line that could cost her money. Every beat 3 now refuses BOTH verdicts and affirms her caution instead, the same stance cards-real-person takes.

🔴 BEAT 4 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 — it must name an OBSTRUCTION, not an absence. Act 1 of the trilogy sells an Energy Clearing Ritual that removes "the shadow that's been blocking your path"; improve-v1/08-clearing-theme-coherence.md found that clearing is SPRUNG at the pitch rather than seeded. This is where it gets seeded, and the object goes between HER and what she wants — the love bucket frames every block as an impersonal thing in her path precisely so removing it blames nobody. "What he never said" cannot be cleared; "what's sitting between you and the words" can.

# `cards-who-hurt-me` — "Why do I still think about someone who hurt me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO DIRECTIVE — 'let him go', 'time to move on', 'try to forget him' are what she has been told by everyone already, and they are not a reading. 🔴 No promise about him and 🔴 no pathologising: obsessed, unhealthy, 'you are stuck' are all banned. 🔴 No timeframe.

⚠️⚠️ THE HEAVIEST HOOK ON THE FUNNEL. She has NAMED A HARM in her own headline, and that pulls two opposite ways: 🔴 never minimise what she named ('he did not mean it', 'it was not that bad', 'perhaps he did not') and 🔴 never convict him either ('he was cruel', 'a narcissist', 'he never loved you') — the second is the tempting one, it feels like taking her side, and it is still a verdict on a real man from a card. Finding: thinking about someone is not the same as excusing them, and she has been treating her own memory as evidence against herself.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the tools are laid out and none of them is put away.
2. You asked why you still think about someone who hurt you. Your hand went to the mind at work.
3. So your mind is still working on it, dear. That's what this shows.
4. And every tool is still out, dear. The job isn't done yet.
5. But thinking of him isn't excusing him, dear. Not for a moment.
6. That's why you are hard on yourself, dear. You read memory as a charge.
7. Let me look closer at what has kept this job open…

</details>

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — one thing holds him and the rest of him is free.
2. You asked why you still think about someone who hurt you. Your hand went to the card that holds one point.
3. So one thing has hold of you, dear. The rest of you moved on.
4. And he's caught at one point only, dear. The rest of him is loose.
5. But from outside, that looks like being stuck, dear.
6. That's why you call yourself stuck, dear, when it's one held point.
7. Let me look closer at what has hold of that one point…

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the ground behind him has fallen away in steps.
2. You asked why you still think about someone who hurt you. Your hand went to the one who kept going.
3. So you kept going, dear. That's the first thing I see.
4. And the ground has gone behind him, dear, and his feet still move.
5. But what happened to you was real, dear, and so is the going on.
6. That's why the two fight in you, dear. Neither cancels the other.
7. Let me look closer at what has been catching at you as you go…

# `cards-wont-commit` — "Why won't he commit to me?"

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands.

On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped ('turned' face-down, 'chose' face-up).

🔴 THESE ASK FOR A PREDICTION OUTRIGHT, which is the exact thing the funnel forbids, so the no-verdict rule runs in BOTH directions plus a no-timeframe rule: 'he will, give it time' reads as a promise and 'he never will' is a pronouncement on a man she loves. 🔴 The bans in tests/tarot-commitment-copy.test.ts are WHOLE-BEAT rather than clause-level, so 'not enough' and 'too much' are unavailable even inside a refusal — the usual absolution form had to be rebuilt without them.

⚠️ THIS HOOK PRESUPPOSES HIS REFUSAL, which opens a failure mode the others do not have: the answer sliding into HER fault. 123 real concerns off this lander show she has usually already started turning the question on herself — 'I feel he's never really tried. Like I'm an option even tho he said different', 'I feel inconsistent energy from him', 'he says he loves me and I believe him but there's something underlying that I can't explain'. So the 'why' is routed to where HE is stuck and left there — never a character verdict, never invented — and what gets affirmed is that her read is sound and the tiredness is earned.

## return-mhf — cards face DOWN (she turned)

### card a — the Magician, the card of will and intention

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Magician, dear. Look — a white band round his dark hair.
2. You asked why he won't commit to you. Your hand went to the man who decides.
3. So the hold on this isn't yours, dear. Nothing you did put it there.
4. And the tools for building are all laid out in front of him, dear.
5. But having the tools is not the same as picking them up, dear.
6. That's why you get the words, dear, and the week after looks the same.
7. Let me look closer at what stands in the way of a choice…

### card b — the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — his arms are out of sight, and his body makes a triangle.
2. You asked why he won't commit to you. Your hand went to the card that stalls.
3. So something has him stuck, dear. You've been feeling exactly that.
4. And his arms are out of sight, dear. You can't see what he's holding.
5. But where he is stuck is his to name, dear. Not mine, and not yours.
6. That's why every reason you land on slides off, dear. None of them stick.
7. Let me look closer at what has this stuck in place…

### card c — the Fool, the card of new beginnings

✅ passes the copy gate · beat 1 is unique on this deck

**NEW — what she would read**

1. You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the little dog has its tail straight up.
2. You asked why he won't commit to you. Your hand went to the one who ties nothing down.
3. So nothing in his life is tied down, dear. You saw that early.
4. And the little dog has its tail straight up, dear. It knows the mood.
5. But loose isn't the same as gone, dear. He still walks beside you.
6. That's why asking for more never turns into a row. It just slides off.
7. Let me look closer at what has been keeping this loose…
